Cedar is the real-time audio\nengine of nkido: a bytecode VM that processes blocks of 128 float samples at\n48 kHz, zero allocations on the audio path. The repository is your current\nworking directory.\n\n## Task\n\nMake these opcodes faster on **x86-64 AVX2** (the gate machine):\n\n- `op_distort_tanh`\n- `op_distort_soft`\n\nThey live in `cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp`. Current source of the opcode bodies:\n\n```cpp\n[[gnu::always_inline]]\ninline void op_distort_tanh(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n    float* out_r = ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.out_buffer + 1));\n    const float* input = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n    const bool stereo_in = (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::STEREO_INPUT) != 0;\n    const float* input_r = stereo_in\n        ? ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.inputs[0] + 1))\n        : nullptr;\n    const float* drive = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[1]);\n    const float* dry_level = (inst.inputs[2] != 0xFFFF) ? ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[2]) : nullptr;\n    const float* wet_level = (inst.inputs[3] != 0xFFFF) ? ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[3]) : nullptr;\n\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) {\n        kernels::distort_tanh_avx2(input, stereo_in ? input_r : input, drive, dry_level,\n                                   wet_level, out_l, out_r);\n        return;\n    }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\n        float d = std::max(0.1f, drive[i]);\n        float dry = drywet::coeff(dry_level, i, 0.0f);\n        float wet = drywet::coeff(wet_level, i, 1.0f);\n        float x_l = input[i];\n        float x_r = stereo_in ? input_r[i] : x_l;\n        out_l[i] = drywet::mix(x_l, std::tanh(x_l * d), dry, wet);\n        out_r[i] = drywet::mix(x_r, std::tanh(x_r * d), dry, wet);\n    }\n}\n\n[[gnu::always_inline]]\ninline void op_distort_soft(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n    float* out_r = ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.out_buffer + 1));\n    const float* input = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n    const bool stereo_in = (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::STEREO_INPUT) != 0;\n    const float* input_r = stereo_in\n        ? ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.inputs[0] + 1))\n        : nullptr;\n    const float* threshold = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[1]);\n    const float* dry_level = (inst.inputs[2] != 0xFFFF) ? ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[2]) : nullptr;\n    const float* wet_level = (inst.inputs[3] != 0xFFFF) ? ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[3]) : nullptr;\n\n    auto soft = [](float x) {\n        if (x > 3.0f) return 1.0f;\n        if (x < -3.0f) return -1.0f;\n        float x2 = x * x;\n        return x * (27.0f + x2) / (27.0f + 9.0f * x2);\n    };\n\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) {\n        kernels::distort_soft_avx2(input, stereo_in ? input_r : input, threshold, dry_level,\n                                   wet_level, out_l, out_r);\n        return;\n    }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\n        float t = std::clamp(threshold[i], 0.1f, 2.0f);\n        float dry = drywet::coeff(dry_level, i, 0.0f);\n        float wet = drywet::coeff(wet_level, i, 1.0f);\n        float x_l = input[i];\n        float x_r = stereo_in ? input_r[i] : x_l;\n        out_l[i] = drywet::mix(x_l, soft(x_l / t) * t, dry, wet);\n        out_r[i] = drywet::mix(x_r, soft(x_r / t) * t, dry, wet);\n    }\n}\n```\n\nCost on the gate machine (median ns per opcode-block, lower is better):\n\n| Opcode | Original scalar | Current (accepted so far) | Speedup so far |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `op_distort_tanh` | 974.44 | 163.28 | 5.97x |\n| `op_distort_soft` | 257.33 | 85.76 | 3.00x |\n\n## Accepted so far (already in the tree)\n\n```diff\ndiff --git a/cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp b/cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp\nindex 4a294b4..b5db34a 100644\n--- a/cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp\n+++ b/cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp\n@@ -6,11 +6,138 @@\n #include \"dsp_state.hpp\"\n #include \"dsp_utils.hpp\"\n #include \"drywet.hpp\"\n+#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n #include <cmath>\n #include <algorithm>\n \n namespace cedar {\n \n+namespace kernels {\n+#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n+\n+// Exp(x) on [-80, 80]: range-reduce to r = x - round(x / ln2) * ln2 and use\n+// a degree-7 Taylor polynomial for exp(r); scale by 2^round(x / ln2).  This\n+// is branchless and accurate enough for the tanh null-test below.\n+CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline __m256 exp_taylor_avx2(__m256 x) noexcept {\n+    const __m256 log2e = _mm256_set1_ps(1.44269504088896341f);\n+    const __m256 half = _mm256_set1_ps(0.5f);\n+    const __m256 ln2_hi = _mm256_set1_ps(0.693359375f);\n+    const __m256 ln2_lo = _mm256_set1_ps(-2.12194440e-4f);\n+    const __m256 hi = _mm256_set1_ps(80.0f);\n+    const __m256 lo = _mm256_set1_ps(-80.0f);\n+\n+    x = _mm256_min_ps(x, hi);\n+    x = _mm256_max_ps(x, lo);\n+\n+    // n = floor(x * log2(e) + 0.5): nearest integer on [-115, 115].\n+    const __m256 fx = _mm256_floor_ps(_mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(x, log2e), half));\n+    __m256 r = _mm256_sub_ps(x, _mm256_mul_ps(fx, ln2_hi));\n+    r = _mm256_sub_ps(r, _mm256_mul_ps(fx, ln2_lo));\n+\n+    // Horner for exp(r) = 1 + r + r^2/2 + ... + r^7/5040, r in [-ln2/2, ln2/2].\n+    __m256 p = _mm256_set1_ps(0.00019841269798041558f);  // 1/5040\n+    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(0.0013888889225199819f));  // 1/720\n+    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(0.0083333337679505348f));  // 1/120\n+    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(0.041666667908430099f));   // 1/24\n+    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(0.1666666716337204f));     // 1/6\n+    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(0.5f));\n+    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f));\n+    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f));\n+\n+    __m256i n = _mm256_cvttps_epi32(fx);\n+    n = _mm256_add_epi32(n, _mm256_set1_epi32(127));\n+    n = _mm256_slli_epi32(n, 23);\n+    return _mm256_mul_ps(p, _mm256_castsi256_ps(n));\n+}\n+\n+// simd: approx tanh \u2014 use exp_taylor_avx2 for tanh(x) = (e^(2x)-1)/(e^(2x)+1),\n+// replacing std::tanh.  Peaks stay well below -100 dBFS on every stimulus.\n+CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline __m256 tanh_exp_avx2(__m256 x) noexcept {\n+    const __m256 one = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f);\n+    x = _mm256_min_ps(x, _mm256_set1_ps(30.0f));\n+    x = _mm256_max_ps(x, _mm256_set1_ps(-30.0f));\n+    const __m256 e = exp_taylor_avx2(_mm256_add_ps(x, x));\n+    return _mm256_div_ps(_mm256_sub_ps(e, one), _mm256_add_ps(e, one));\n+}\n+\n+// simd: approx tanh \u2014 tanh_exp_avx2 above, used instead of std::tanh.\n+CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void distort_tanh_avx2(const float* in_l, const float* in_r,\n+                            float* out_r) noexcept {\n+    const __m256 v0p1 = _mm256_set1_ps(0.1f);\n+    const __m256 v1 = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f);\n+    const __m256 v0 = _mm256_setzero_ps();\n+\n+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8) {\n+        const __m256 d = _mm256_max_ps(_mm256_load_ps(drive + i), v0p1);\n+        const __m256 dry = dry_level ? _mm256_load_ps(dry_level + i)\n+                                     : _mm256_setzero_ps();\n+        const __m256 wet = wet_level ? _mm256_load_ps(wet_level + i) : v1;\n+        const __m256 x_l = _mm256_load_ps(in_l + i);\n+        const __m256 x_r = _mm256_load_ps(in_r + i);\n+\n+        const __m256 y_l = tanh_exp_avx2(_mm256_mul_ps(x_l, d));\n+        const __m256 y_r = tanh_exp_avx2(_mm256_mul_ps(x_r, d));\n+\n+        const __m256 out_lv = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(x_l, dry),\n+                                            _mm256_mul_ps(y_l, wet));\n+        const __m256 out_rv = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(x_r, dry),\n+ You may only edit: `cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/**`, `cedar/include/cedar/dsp/simd*.hpp`, `cedar/src/dsp/simd*.cpp`. Any other edit (benchmarks, tests,\n   experiments, CMake, scripts) rejects the whole attempt.\n2. **Never delete or change the scalar body.** It is the oracle and the\n   fallback; the verifier runs it with SIMD disabled and requires\n   bit-identical output to the original.\n3. Output must be **bit-identical** to the original for every opcode.\n   Exception: op_distort_tanh call `tanh`/`tan`; for these only, an approximation is allowed if (a) you mark it with a `// simd: approx` comment naming the replaced function, and (b) its peak error stays at or below -100 dBFS (about 1e-5 absolute) on every stimulus.\n4. The final verification also runs every opcode on **randomised stimuli you\n   cannot see** (different signals, levels down to denormals, parameter\n   trajectories). A kernel that only works for the stimuli in the benchmark\n   source fails there.\n5. No allocation, locks or syscalls in a kernel.     // 1/6\n    43\t    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(0.5f));\n    44\t    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f));\n    45\t    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f));\n    46\t\n    47\t    __m256i n = _mm256_cvttps_epi32(fx);\n    48\t    n = _mm256_add_epi32(n, _mm256_set1_epi32(127));\n    49\t    n = _mm256_slli_epi32(n, 23);\n    50\t    return _mm256_mul_ps(p, _mm256_castsi256_ps(n));\n    51\t}\n    52\t\n    53\t// simd: approx tanh \u2014 use exp_taylor_avx2 for tanh(x) = (e^(2x)-1)/(e^(2x)+1),\n    54\t// replacing std::tanh.  Peaks stay well below -100 dBFS on every stimulus.\n    55\tCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline __m256 tanh_exp_avx2(__m256 x) noexcept {\n    56\t    const __m256 one = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f);\n    57\t    x = _mm256_min_ps(x, _mm256_set1_ps(30.0f));\n    58\t    x = _mm256_max_ps(x, _mm256_set1_ps(-30.0f));\n    59\t    const __m256 e = exp_taylor_avx2(_mm256_add_ps(x, x));\n    60\t    return _mm256_div_ps(_mm256_sub_ps(e, one), _mm256_add_ps(e, one));\n    61\t}\n    62\t\n    63\t// simd: approx tanh \u2014 tanh_exp_avx2 above, used instead of std::tanh.\n    64\tCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void distort_tanh_avx2(const float* in_l, const float* in_r,\n    65\t                                                const float* drive, const float* dry_level,\n    66\t                                                const float* wet_level, float* out_l,\n    67\t                                                float* out_r) noexcept {\n    68\t    const __m256 v0p1 = _mm256_set1_ps(0.1f);\n    69\t    const __m256 v1 = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f);\n    70\t    const __m256 v0 = _mm256_setzero_ps();\n    71\t\n    72\t    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8) {\n    73\t        const 83\t        const __m256 out_lv = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(x_l, dry),\n    84\t                                            _mm256_mul_ps(y_l, wet));\n    85\t        const __m256 out_rv = _mm256_store_ps(out_r + i, out_rv);\n    89\t    }\n    90\t}\n    91\t\n    92\t// Exact soft-clip shape from the scalar body: a clamped cubic rational with\n    93\t// branches only for |x| > 3, _mm256_min_ps(_mm256_max_ps(x, _mm256_set1_ps(-3.0f)), v3);\n    99\t    const __m256 x2 = _mm256_mul_ps(xc, xc);\n   100\t    const __m256 num = _mm256_mul_ps(xc, _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_set1_ps(27.0f), distort_soft_avx2(const float* in_l, const float* in_r,\n   108\t                                                const float* threshold,\n   109\t                                                const float* dry_level, const float* wet_level,\n   110\t                                                float* out_l, float* out_r) noexcept {\n   111\t    const __m256 vlo = _mm256_set1_ps(0.1f);\n ============================================================================\n   142\t// DISTORT_TANH: Tanh Saturation\n   143\t// 271\t}\n   272\t\n   273\t// ============================================================================\n   274\t// DISTORT_FOLD: Wavefolder with ADAA (Antiderivative Antialiasing)\n   275\t// Every seed is guaranteed one\n    78\t    // near-denormal noise segment (1e-35..1e-25) and one hot one (2-4x),\n    79\t    // at random positions, so that coverage never depends on luck.\n    80\t    void make_hidden(std::mt19937& rng) {\n    81\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n    82\t        constexpr std::size_t seg = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE / 8;\n    83\t        const auto tiny_seg = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 8.0f) % 8;\n    84\t        const auto hot_seg = (tiny_seg + 1 + static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 7.0f) % 7) % 8;\n    85\t        for (std::size_t s = 0; s < 8; ++s) {\n    86\t            int kind = static_cast<int>(u(rng) * 4.0f);\n    87\t            float level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -30.0f * u(rng) * u(rng)) * (1.0f + 3.0f * u(rng));\n    88\t            if (s == tiny_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -25.0f - 10.0f * u(rng)); }\n    89\t            if (s == hot_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * (2.0f + 2.0f * u(rng)); }\n    90\t            float dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f);\n    91\t            for (std::size_t k = s * seg; k < (s + 1) * seg; ++k) {\n    92\t                switch (kind) {\n    93\t                    case 0: noise[k] *= level; break;\n    94\t                    case 1: noise[k] = u(rng) < 0.002f ? level * (u(rng) < 0.5f ? -1.0f : 1.0f) : 0.0f; break;\n    95\t                    case 2: noise[k] = (k % 4096 == 0) ? (dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f)) : dc; break;\n    96\t                    default: noise[k] = 0.0f; break;\n    97\t                }\n    98\t            }\n    99\t        }\n   100\t    }\n   101\t    float operator()(std::size_t n) const {\n   102\t        if (g_seed) return noise[n];\n   103\t        constexpr std::size_t total = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE;\n   104\t        constexpr std::size_t quarter = total / 4;\n   105\t        std::size_t seg = n / quarter, k = n % quarter;\n   106\t        float t = static_cast<float>(k) / static_cast<float>(quarter);\n   107\t        switch (seg) {\n   108\t            case 0: return gain * noise[n];\n   109\t            case 1: {\n   110\t                // 20 Hz \u2192 20 kHz exponential sweep, phase-integrated\n   111\t                double f0 = 20.0, f1 = 20000.0, T = double(quarter) / double(SR);\n   112\t                double tt = double(k) / double(SR);\n   113\t                double kk = std::log(f1 / f0) / T;\n   114\t                double ph = 2.0 * 3.14159265358979 * f0 * (std::exp(kk * tt) - 1.0) / kk;\n   115\t                return gain * static_cast<float>(std::sin(ph));\n   116\t            }\n   117\t            case 2: return (k % 997 == 0) ? gain : 0.0f;\n   118\t            default: return gain * (2.0f * t - 1.0f);\n   119\t        }\n   120\t    }\n   121\t};\n   122\t\n   123\tStim ramp(float lo, float hi, std::size_t period) {\n   124\t    if (g_seed) {  // hidden: same range, random rate and phase\n   125\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n   126\t        auto p = static_cast<std::size_t>(static_cast<float>(period) * (0.05f + 3.0f * u(g_hidden_rng))) + 1;\n   127\t        auto off = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(g_hidden_rng) * static_cast<float>(p));\n   128\t        return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n + off, p); };\n   129\t    }\n   130\t    return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n, period); };\n   131\t}\n   132\tStim constant(float v) { return [=](std::size_t) { return v; }; }\n   133\t\n   134\tstruct Case {\n   135\t    const char* name;\n   136\t    Opcode op;\n   137\t    std::vector<Stim> inputs; Each audio opcode opts in by setting the flag (eventually all of them); the allocator reserves an adjacent buffer pair (`R = L + 1`); the opcode body processes L and R inside one call with shared state.
